### Ecclesiastical Domicile Main Building - The Cathedral
ecclesiastical_domicile_main_01 = {
	slot_type = main
	internal_slots = 2
	construction_time = @ecclesiastical_domicile_construction_time_main
	allowed_domicile_types = { ecclesiastical_domicile }

	cost = {
		treasury_or_gold = ecclesiastical_domicile_building_cost_main_01_value
	}

	character_modifier = {
		domicile_monthly_gold_add = @ecclesiastical_domicile_main_domicile_monthly_gold_add_1
		domicile_monthly_piety_add = @ecclesiastical_domicile_domicile_monthly_piety_add_main_01

	}

		tax_mult = @ecclesiastical_domicile_tax_mult
	}

	ai_value = {

	}

ecclesiastical_domicile_main_02 = {
	slot_type = main
	internal_slots = 3
	construction_time = @ecclesiastical_domicile_construction_time_main
	allowed_domicile_types = { ecclesiastical_domicile }
	previous_building = ecclesiastical_domicile_main_01
	
	can_construct = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = great_project_grand_cathedral_required

				has_building_or_higher = great_project_cathedral_01
			}
		}
	}

# CATHEDRAL INTERNAL UPGRADES #

#Masons' Lodge
cathedral_masons_lodge_01 = {
	construction_time = @ecclesiastical_domicile_construction_time_internal_01
	allowed_domicile_types = { ecclesiastical_domicile }
	slot_type = internal
	previous_building = ecclesiastical_domicile_main_01

	can_construct = {
		domicile ?= {
			has_domicile_building_or_higher = ecclesiastical_domicile_main_01
		}
	}

	cost = {
		treasury_or_gold = ecclesiastical_domicile_building_cost_internal_01_value
	}

	character_modifier = {
		domicile_build_gold_cost = @ecclesiastical_domicile_build_gold_cost
		domicile_build_speed = @ecclesiastical_domicile_domicile_build_speed
	}

	ai_value = {
		#Outranks the external buildings' 9 so the Lodge's build discounts are in place before the rest of the domicile goes up
		value = 10
		add = ecclesiastical_domicile_building_ai_modifier_value
	}

	asset = {
		# trigger = { # Warcraft
		# 	domicile_location.county.rite = {
		# 		rite_has_doctrine = special_doctrine_is_eastern_christian_faith
		# 	}
		# }
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_mason.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/estate_building_temple_christian_orthodox.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/estate_building_temple_christian_orthodox_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/domicile_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_workshop_mason"
	}
	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_mason.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/estate_building_temple_christian.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/estate_building_temple_christian_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/domicile_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_workshop_mason"
	}
}

### Cloister
cloister_01 = {
	construction_time = @ecclesiastical_domicile_construction_time_external_01
	internal_slots = 2
	allowed_domicile_types = { ecclesiastical_domicile }
	can_construct = {
		#There must be a monastic order in the archdiocese your domicile is within
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = cloister_build_existing_order_options_invalid
			root.capital_county.clerical_region_title ?= {
				any_county_in_clerical_region = {
					any_county_province = {
						barony = { is_leased_out = yes }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cost = {
		treasury_or_gold = ecclesiastical_domicile_building_cost_external_01_value
	}

	parameters = {
		estate_cloister_take_vows = yes
		estate_cloister_monastic_movement = yes
		estate_increase_physician_aptitude_1 = yes
	}

	character_modifier = {
		piety_standalone_gain_mult = 0.05
		stress_gain_mult = @ecclesiastical_domicile_stress_gain_mult_external
		domicile_monthly_piety_add = @ecclesiastical_domicile_piety_add_external
		domicile_monthly_gold_add = @ecclesiastical_domicile_gold_add_external
	}
	
	ai_value = {
		value = 9
		add = ecclesiastical_domicile_building_ai_modifier_value

palace_synodal_hall_01 = {
	construction_time = @ecclesiastical_domicile_construction_time_internal_01
	allowed_domicile_types = { ecclesiastical_domicile }
	slot_type = internal
	previous_building = episcopal_palace_01

		domicile ?= {
			has_domicile_building_or_higher = episcopal_palace_01
		}
	}

		# 	}
		# }
